2026-10-12 | Space/GNC | (note) | ENG-07c bracket, n 4.8, 6.6 V both-stall plug voltage (as-drawn chain), 500 runs, same draws. ENG-07b (longest saturation episode in the landing burn, p99 over runs) is 60 ms at J_int 1.0e-3 kg m2 but 141 ms at 1.5e-3 (fails). P(landing) holds to 4e-3 (0.980, 2 lost / 1 gained vs AD_prov_2kg, p 1) and loses from 5e-3: 0.960 (11/0, p 0.001), 6e-3 0.936, 8e-3 0.920, 12e-3 0.818. At 7.6 V the boundary barely moves: 4e-3 0.980, 8e-3 0.936 (loses). So ENG-07c = 1e-3 kg m2 stands, set by ENG-07b; P(landing) alone would allow 4e-3. n 6 on the same supply loses already at 4e-3 (0.958, 13/1, p 0.002). Model caveat: J_int n^2 is added on the engine side of the servo-stiffness spring; if the servo's quoted response already contains its rotor, this double-counts (B-TVC-1 decides). Electronics replays Combustion's pool source through the SIL plant. (4) Rounded port entrance r >= 4 mm is now REQUIRED on every grain, flight and stand (decomposition peak 40.6 vs 43.7 bar abs, MEOP 45). Bleed path set by TOTAL flow ~31 g/s (1.0 mm-equivalent; needed Tp 1.8-1.9 s; scheduled 2.5 s); 0.8 mm per tap (40 g/s) not adopted; 1.0 mm per tap never. GNC's <= 0.8 mm (~20 g/s) is NOT adopted: a 15.6 g/s path fails the sweep, and the sweep is the A1 blocker. The stuck-open consequence at 31.3 g/s (GNC S313_0 A1 0.896 additive) is handled as risk: two NC valves in series; bleed drivers hardware-inhibited outside the scheduled purge window (whole B1 flight, A1 ascent, unless CF-4 shows the pad start needs a purge); FMEA on the double/common-cause fault; research FSW leak response. If closure is not confirmed before E1 the relight proceeds and BLEED_FAULT goes to the FC (a leaking relight beats no relight). GNC re-fly checks the A1 reserve at 76 g (PS10 had 2+2 exhausted draws); if exhaustion remains, line the segment (saves ~0.3 s) or trim A1 apogee. (6) Bleed path: F1 the restriction must control: each valve >= 4x the restriction's flow capacity (seat >= 2 mm or Kv verified at 55 bar); Burkert 6027 HP 1.0 mm does not qualify; merged with OI-BLEED-COIL (12 V NC, peak-and-hold). F2 no unfiltered path to the pintle seat during the burn: check valve on the T4 leg (T4 -> tee only) or an inline <= 15 um element on the FD-01 leg, Mechanical chooses. F3 a restrictor at each tap, areas summing to the 1.0 mm equivalent. Combustion re-runs the purge grid on the final path areas. (7) Mechanical publishes the file behind S11's 15.91 cm3 | lead

2026-10-13 | lead | (decision) | On REV-GNC-3 (line 180; SIM only): (1) gnc.json v3.1 is NOT published from a5564b45ed7fb463. GNC rebuilds the staging as rev2.1 (RV3-01 (a)-(f): superseded markers, line 175 and this line folded in, corrected G basis, RG-16 detector-output results, V probes, named comparators RV3-09, bit-check record RV3-08); an independent document check follows; then v3.1 is published. No new flights are needed for that. (2) Late commit (RV3-02): the basis "a T_fire delay <= 0.6 s costs no measurable P" is WITHDRAWN. The rule is restated as the P_total-maximising deadline: PURGE commits if the lead to the plain relight trigger is >= L*, else no purge; L* = 1.7 s if an unpurged start is LO (break-even against P_total ~0.75) and L* = 1.3 s if HI (skipping gives P_total 0). FSW interim value 1.7 s until Combustion settles LO/HI and the unpurged-start hazard (line 175 (3) refutation clause stands). "The 0.81 s plan holds" is scoped to the predictor path. GNC flies the contingency (flows 1.9-2.4 s) with the L400 plateau and estimates its probability by gate-failure injection; canopy dynamics remain TO-VERIFY. (3) RG-16 (RV3-03): "per-flight form is provisional support" is WITHDRAWN. PROVISIONAL detection-latency allowance: 0.5 s from the onset of the 670 N plateau, strict windowed form (2.973 % vs 2.985 %: a pass with almost no margin on an ASSUMED white vibration model, so not robust); no window-gain credit. GNC computes the trajectory consequence of 0.5 s at 670 N (delta-v, apex, landing P) on A1 and B1 and the B1 miss-side budget under this definition; if the consequence is unacceptable or a measured vibration spectrum removes the margin, the detector changes, not the allowance. FC_THRUST stays NOT armed on A1; arming on any mission needs that budget. A1 FMEA records: no over-thrust detector after an in-flight Pc loss (the 30 bar abort needs Pc valid) (RV3-12). (4) Drogue release (RV3-04): R2 is ADOPTED in design over R1. pcf_tmax stays 0.5 s (the flown value; L400 releases 440-470 ms, 0 aborts); 0.8 s is NOT adopted. GNC R2 v2: re-reference the baseline at E2 or compensate drogue drag, re-derive the threshold for the window, fly both bases at 0.5 and 0.8 s with L400, pcf_k 0.875, flame-out, onsets 0.005/0.010 s and the landings after 0.5-0.8 s releases, margins reported for the full wind distribution and inside W10 < 6 m/s; the lead then sets pcf_tmax. Combustion bounds cold-flow apparent thrust under the drogue after a flame-out. (5) Mass (RV3-05): the 0.30 kg trigger is REPLACED: GNC re-flies at the actual ledger for any confirmed landing-mass growth >= 0.10 kg over GNC ledger v3d, summed across all engine-module and vehicle changes (including line 177 (1) TC-09 IN718 and (4) bearings, and the bleed valve pair). GNC flies +0.1/+0.2/+0.3 kg at 285 m to replace the linear estimate. Mechanical line 178 already reports the v3.5 fixed group at +0.764 kg (0.60 kg of it ASSUMED bleed valves), so the trigger is expected to be met: GNC re-flies at the v3.5 ledger once massprops_v3.5 is handed back and compared with v3d; the bleed valve pair selection is mass-critical. (6) 285 m stays ADOPTED with conditions (RV3-06): the launch-commit wind rule W10 < 6 m/s AND sigma_u(3 m) <= 1.0 m/s becomes a HARD rule for A1 (was provisional, line 161); GNC flies 285 m on all four bases and on pl, e19, the prep path, L400 and PURGE NOT RUN before SIL/HIL freeze. (7) K (RV3-10): GNC flies the k_hat re-reference replacement on FSW-R2 with a throttle-map error band before SIL/HIL freeze. (8) Configuration (RV3-07/08): GNC pins the simulator data tables and work-area inputs, records code hashes in every bit-check run, and makes P30_reproduced part of the G6 pass criterion. (9) B1 default grain gate (RV3-11) stays (a_meas >= 1.25 -> change grain; 27.2 % of draws in the GNC model); a printed ABS grain is the cost; revisited when T1-T4 measure a. (10) Interface note (RV3-12): the <= 73.0 g / <= 76 g purge figure is vented, vehicle-level N2O; tank liquid depletion is 81.6 g (8.6 g boils into the ullage); reserves already include it; tank-outlet coverage checks use liquid depletion. (11) Next GNC session launched (max): rev2.1 first, then items (2)-(8). None of this is physical approval | lead

Mechanical classifies it on the critical stress-classification line: primary membrane and bending against the allowable at temperature, P+Q range against shakedown (2 Sy(T)), and low-cycle fatigue for 50 hot-fire cycles (ASSUMED test plus flight count) with factors of 2 on strain range or 20 on cycles, whichever governs. (2) 608 bearings: line 177 (4) stands with Mechanical's derived requirement C0 >= 1.80 kN per bearing from a maker datasheet (maker, part number, C0 cited); then re-run the gimbal and TVC checks. (6) J-FWD with A4-80 is re-run in joints.py (not scaled) before freeze. 2026-10-13 | lead | (decision) | Lead read of the stored max-thrust FEA tables. Sources: 04-fea/structural/results/sax_margin_table_L1_TC09IN718_thJ1revB_TC09IN_v1.csv (ba26b3d7a78cc753) and s3d_margin_table_v1.1.csv. These negatives were not in the Mechanical handback summary (line 176 covered FA-1 only); all values are SIM at SF 1.5/2.0 unless stated. LC1 steady max thrust 500 N (also LC2, TS1a/b, BN): TC-02 head 6061-T6 MS -0.75 (ult -0.76), P+Q -0.24 (no elastic shakedown), 215 C against a 150 C usable limit, thermal-driven at r 7 z 106.6; TC-03 IN718 MS -0.37 (ult -0.47), primary +34.9, P+Q +0.90, 600 C against 650 C; NZ-01 graphite throat insert brittle MS -0.44 (thermal, s1 against St, SF 2.0); LC3 max gimbal (3D): GM-01/02 gimbal ring MS_y -0.53 (6061-T6), and with the adopted 7075-T6 still MS_y -0.056 and MS_u -0.19 at the worst pose. Decisions: (1) Criterion for thermal (secondary) stress in metal parts, adopted for all cases: accept when primary MS >= 0 at SF 1.5/2.0, AND P+Q range <= 2 Fty(T) (elastic shakedown), AND low-cycle fatigue life >= 50 hot cycles with factors of 2 on strain range or 20 on cycles, AND part temperature <= its usable limit; otherwise DESIGN CHANGE. TC-03 passes the first two; Mechanical shows LCF. TC-02 fails shakedown and its temperature limit, so DESIGN CHANGE is mandatory; line 182 (1) covers it, and the fix must clear LC1, LC2, TS1a/b, BN and TB70 together. (2) NZ-01 graphite throat insert: no design change if Mechanical shows the insert is mechanically captured (fragments of a cracked insert cannot leave the nozzle stack and the throat area change stays bounded). Acceptance is then TEST-BASED (cracked-but-contained HYPOTHESIS): inspect the insert after every static fire; spare inserts in the BOM; refuted by any fragment loss or throat-area change > 2 %. If capture cannot be shown, DESIGN CHANGE (grade or geometry). (3) GM-01/02: DESIGN CHANGE (geometry) to MS_y >= 0 and MS_u >= 0 at SF 1.5/2.0 at every LC3 pose in 7075-T6, with the mass delta. (4) Mechanical reports the minimum MS of every part for every case (no case left out of the handback summary), and the FEA deliverable for the founder shows these negatives as found until fixed | lead

Candidates to list first: pintle and servo linkage, equipment on the forward closure (FS-01/MV-P, bleed valves), nozzle on the J1 lip, Pc port and sense line. SF 1.0 as in 188 (9). mechanical.json v2.2 carries the FA-1 entry with this domain (amends 186 (4)). A part that fails at its period is a DESIGN CHANGE item, or Mechanical shows its period from the modal model. (2) R3-03: the 1.8 kN x 4 ms thrust rectangle is the load only for parts with T <= 20 ms (REQUIREMENTS l.118 'low-frequency parts' corrected); for T > 20 ms (vehicle modes, equipment on isolators, tank mounts) the FA-1 long-period thrust spectrum (reviewer DERIVED: 1.76 kN at 25 ms, 1.45 kN at 40 ms, 1.04 kN at 100 ms, whole history; pool plateau ASSUMED) is the load. Combustion publishes it in v2.4b; the lead carries it into ICD-AIRFRAME v2.1 for the airframe owner. (3) R3-01: the 'E2 + 0.22 s' of 186 (1) is WITHDRAWN. The unpurged alternative is flown by GNC only as a comparator with the population hold table: H >= 0.30 s with the 2500 N/s ramp to 200 N flown during the hold, H >= 0.19 s with the ramp to 500 N, and no admissible hold with flow held at the start lift. Because 186 (2) holds the command at the start level while the drogue is attached with Pc invalid, the alternative is not admissible with Pc invalid. GNC counts the residual released-then-A3 class from Combustion's per-record classes (v2.4b) and the P cost of the longer hold (REPORT l.1727). The baseline of 186 (1) is unchanged: below L* = 1.3 s no purge and no unpurged relight; the adoption rule is unchanged. (4) R3-05/R3-14: the 186 (2) command hold stays ADOPTED; its 16.31 N margin is stated as CONDITIONAL on Cd x A x G at the start lift. CF-3 adds a start-lift point: mdot and load-cell F at the start-level lift with the encoder lift recorded; acceptance F <= 16.31 N minus the heat allowance, or mdot <= 52.6-56.3 g/s; a failure returns the hold level and the W10 rule to the lead. The heat TO-VERIFY moves from chamber heat pick-up to injector-body and feed soak-back temperature at the relight (heat at tank pressure, threshold 2.8-3.7 kW). GNC's R2 v2 uses F_cold plus the grain_q1d start-level lit history (fuel-starved 0.1-0.4 s at 0.2039 mm), not 62.5 N x pcf_k. (5) R3-15/R3-16: 186 (3) reads '... more than 2 % beyond the erosion predicted for that firing, the prediction frozen before the firing and updated only with earlier firings'. 186 (5) reads 'quasi-static coverage needs 13.6 mm of liquid (78.6-82.5 g); 766 g remains at the end of the purge flow, 9.3-9.7x'. (6) R3-17: GNC checks the landing usable reserve against the outlet dip onset (60-62 g at 125 N, 68-69 g at 250 N, 78-80 g at 500 N; DERIVED) instead of the ASSUMED 3 % (60 g) residual; Mechanical's OI-32 outlet geometry closes it. (7) R3-10: the stand dynamic Pc channel for IGN-1 and the T series is >= 100 bar abs with a stated proof rating (founder instrumentation item); Electronics states the 070BG flight transducer's over-range behaviour and its effect on the 30 bar abort, the Pc-valid flag and the logged FA-1 peak. (8) R3-06/R3-07/R3-08: the T1-T4 flux test becomes two-sided (shown only if |S - S_ox| <= 1.645 sigma; S below that is 'neither shown': the default gate stays and Combustion re-evaluates the burn limit on the measured axial profile). T1-T4 and CF-4 gain red lines and abort criteria, validity rules for short firings, the IS-1/IS-2 preparation, the continuous-liquid probe threshold, the char-removal procedure, counts and N2O budgets (T1 at 2t = 8 s uses 1.93 kg: state the stand tank capacity or shorten t) and a TO-QUOTE line (Sønderborg DK-6400). CF-4 is re-baselined on the frozen v3.5.1 bleed path. (9) R3-09/R3-11: v2.4b folds line 186 and this line, removes the stale text, and recomputes the H19 lip-melt bound for IN718 with a datasheet melting range (the recalled 1260-1336 C is TO-VERIFY). The Pc port is close-coupled so the transducer path period is <= 1 ms (then 102 bar ultimate < 141 bar proof), shown by Mechanical with geometry and period; if that cannot be met, Electronics selects a flight transducer with proof >= the spectrum value at the line period and states the resolution cost. 070BG saturation, recovery and post-over-range shift stay MISSING: founder B-PC-1 over-range row on a spare unit (70-100 bar g, below the 140 bar g proof) and an inquiry to TE. GNC re-verifies the window on the FE plant (RV6-14 re-run (a)-(e)) before the datasheet quotes it as design-to; until then the datasheet quotes it with its plant basis. gnc.json airframe_first_bending_min_Hz 10 Hz (goal 15) is recorded NOT CONSISTENT with ENG-07d at this window with one fixed notch: 10-22 Hz fail on at least one axis, 24.3-30 Hz pass (DERIVED). This is an open item for the airframe work (line 222); the engine-side remedy (second notch on the measured bending mode) stays a GNC study. (4) Landing mass, RV6-02 ADOPTED. The lead restates the upper bound: nominal planning touchdown mass <= 16.15 kg (the v3.5.1 estimate, covered by the 210 m decision Monte Carlo, N=1000 per main base); a planning mass above 16.15 kg needs its own paired arm. The lower bounds are unchanged. The dispersed 14.18-18.13 kg is a record only, with P(80 g) 0.976 at >= 16.15 kg against 0.989 below (Fisher p 0.049). (5) R2 starve window: 0.8 s ADOPTED for the W10 < 6 m/s launch-commit envelope. GNC shows >= 0.20 s margin over the 0.4 s top of the starve range (0.5 s has about 0 to -0.1 s), and 0/1502 dead-vehicle releases at W10 < 6; 4/2072 at all winds are outside the hard wind rule. Scope per RV6-08: SIM, N=60, A1_none pw, 285 m v3d, transfer ASSUMED, thresholds 0.30 / 0.60 s (0.65 s at W10 < 6). Electronics v2.11 carries 0.8 s into its S17 sweep and confirms it or returns it to the lead. (6) Mode-3 estimator: ADOPTED as a flight-software requirement. Electronics states the FC frame-noise budget against the +5.3 to +6.0 N mode-3 margin: at 5 N rms (iid ASSUMED) the bounding run releases with probability 6.5-18.4 % (RV6-07, DERIVED). (7) RE-24, lead safety decision: the GIMBAL_FAULT abort is NOT inhibited near touchdown; a detected landing-burn jam aborts as designed. Why: continuing gains landing P 0.136-0.150 against 0.084-0.088 only inside a failure whose rate is unknown; a thrust cut gives a more predictable impact point than a lit engine held at a jammed angle; and an inhibit adds a mode to verify. Revisit if B-TVC-1 gives a jam rate. The undetected-jam result carries the RV6-06 scope (31 runs per base and servo, all late jams, 0 lost / 0 gained, one-sided UB95 9.2 % per arm); 0/1000 false aborts (UB95 0.30 %; encoder noise, vibration and supply sag not modelled). (8) Gate delay, RV6-03 condition ADOPTED: the 210 m relight P is used for flight acceptance only after (a) a gate-delay injection arm at 210 m (D = 0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0 s, both states, N=1000 per main base, line-208 statistics) and (b) a bound on the drogue deploy-to-inflation delay (TO-VERIFY, canopy data). It is not needed for v3.2 publication or the datasheet; it is a GNC open item. (9) Line 213's 'A1 duty cycle shortens' is corrected per RV6-05 (not supported: burn p99 8.443 / 8.270 s at 210 m against 8.446 / 8.227 s at 285 m); B1 governs sizing. Payload record per RV6-09; the 2.20 kg option's A1 burn p99 8.92 / 8.71 s (max 9.24 s) exceeds the 8.258 s sizing counter, so its B1, thermal and erosion check is TO-VERIFY and the datasheet says so. (b) Preload treatment of the J-FWD screws (a bearing-type joint, see (c)): at LC2 (chamber MEOP 45 bar, SF 1.5 / 2.0, part temperature) the screw must pass yield as a combined von Mises of the MAXIMUM installation preload (+25 %) on the stress area and the factored shear on the minor area against k_T Rp0.2, and ultimate as Rt^2 + Rs^2 <= 1 with the maximum preload (NASA-STD-5020A form, AS RECALLED, TO-VERIFY); at FA1S (abnormal, 188 (9) factors) the ultimate interaction with the maximum preload must pass, and the yield check may stay shear-only on the minor area only with the preload-relaxation argument stated and the 233 (4) inspect-or-replace rule (all HW-101 replaced after any Pc above 60 bar abs). The reviewer's v3.5.1 8x M5 LC2 combined check is +0.22 / +0.07 / -0.08 (nominal RT / nominal hot / +25 % hot) [DERIVED]. Mechanical re-checks the v3.5.2 10x M5 on this basis; if LC2 fails, the cheapest fix comes first (a lower installation torque with a thread locker fit for the joint's temperature, method M-T1L, stating the preload range and its effect on the TC-02 internal-thread shear and on retention) before any hardware change. (c) Friction capacity is below the joint load at LC2 and FA1S (v3.5.1: 8.85 kN against 11.6 / 38.8 kN, mu 0.2 ASSUMED), so the screws act as shear pins: hole bearing, fretting and wear belong in the sizing and in the inspection rule; Mechanical states a gauge interval for the TC-01 holes and TC-02 taps and a HW-101 replacement interval in firings (ASSUMED until a test). (e) LC2 joins FA1S in the 231 (3) contact sub-model, with the HW-101 preload modelled at nominal and +25 %. (2) RM2-02: there is no factor conflict. LC2 = chamber MEOP 45 bar on the pressure parts, SF 1.5 / 2.0 at part temperature (ENG-09, loadcases_v1 LC2, 188 (9) nominal list); LC2F = the 907.7 N failure thrust on the structure, SF 1.0 / 1.5 (188 (9) abnormal list); FEA3D's F2 LC2F column stands as published. 231 (4) is CORRECTED to 'LC2 rows (MEOP 45 bar, SF 1.5 / 2.0)'; the reviewer's interim LC2F re-post is superseded. FEA3D fixes post_conv.py gci() (no order and no GCI when |e32/e21| < 1) and republishes f1_convergence.json. (8) TC-01 WALL AT THE J-FWD HOLES (08:40Z, on line 237): the preload pull on the case wall is SECONDARY. The preload is imposed by tightening (a displacement), wall yielding relaxes it, and the wall's inward travel is bounded by the fit gap, after which the plug carries the load; there is no collapse path (self-limiting; ASME VIII-2 Part 5 definition, AS RECALLED). The wall's primary check is pressure + inertia + the screw shear-bearing reaction (FA1S hole bearing, ligament Pm, shear-out; pass). Conditions: (a) P+Q <= 2 Fty(T) at the soak at both band ends, at 3750 N and at the 4096 N J-F1 maximum; (b) relaxation: the shell force capped at first yield at each frame's temperature, the excess carried as permanent set, the preload loss added to the embedding, and the no-slack check repeated at 0.040 / 0.059 / 0.078 for 2250 and 4096 N installations, basis and sensitivity embedding, over two firings; first yield is taken on the seat line (conservative), and the ring-line bound only if the check fails there; (c) LCF passes; (d) OPEN with T-F1.
